Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the post-quantum space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 142 and the convergence rate is O(728/n^3).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by post-quantum generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 142, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 728. QED.
